A budding yeast that is homozygous recessive for <span style="color: #436b14;">Gene C</span> grows in dense, irregular clusters, with cells clumping together rather than spreading smoothly.</li> <li><strong><span style="color: #8f308b;">Gene Y</span></strong> is associated with the '<span style="color: #8f308b;"><i>yolk</i></span>' phenotype.
